## From the manufacturer

![Image 80: GEARWRENCH Logo Forge Ahead](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 82: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)  

![Image 84: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
##### About GEARWRENCH

G EARWRENCH produced the original five-degree ratcheting wrench in 1996 and quickly became a go-to brand for professional automotive technicians. The innovation didn’t stop there. Year after year, the GEARWRENCH brand continues to expand its line—in ratcheting tools and beyond—to help automotive and industrial technicians get their jobs done faster and easier.

### GEARWRENCH 89058 Mechanic's Tool Set

GEARWRENCH mechanics tools have all been designed with the professional user in mind to increase productivity, access, and strength. The ratchets, sockets, and wrenches are made from premium alloy steel & feature a full polish chrome finish that resists rust and corrosion. This set is perfect for the automotive or industrial technician who wants a ratchet and sockets for precision work in confined spaces.

*    Durable heavy-duty blow-molded carrying case with easy to read markings 
*    Includes most commonly used SAE and metric sizes 

### GEARWRENCH 84T Ratchets

![Image 86: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The finely-geared 84-tooth full polish chrome ratchets turn fasteners with as small as 4.3° of swing arc allowing you to complete the job faster.

![Image 88: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The low-profile teardrop shaped head of the GEARWRENCH 84T ratchets provide greater access in tight work areas.

![Image 90: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

Durable blow mold case for tool retention & storage.

### GEARWRENCH Sockets

![Image 92: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The off-corner loading design of the GEARWRENCH ratcheting wrenches provides a stronger grip on fasteners, as well as, reduces fastener rounding.

![Image 94: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

Large, hard-stamped markings allow for easy identification and can be easily recognized as SAE (double line) or metric (knurled line).

![Image 96: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

The camfered socket openings allow the user to guide the socket onto fasteners.

### GEARWRENCH 12-Point Combination Wrenches

![Image 98: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### More Torque

The open end of the GEARWRENCH combination wrenches features an off-corner loading design that gives a stronger grip on fasteners, reduces fastener rounding, and gives the user up to 25% more torque.

![Image 100: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Quick Identification

The large size stamp on the open end of the GEARWRENCH combination wrenches allow the user to quickly and easily identify the size they need.

![Image 102: Wrench, Gear, Gear Wrench, Gearwrench, Ratchet, Ratcheting Wrench](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Greater Access

The GEARWRENCH combination wrenches has a 15° offset on the box end providing the user greater clearance to avoid hitting their knuckles.

## Product Description

GEARWRENCH mechanics tools have all been designed with the professional user in mind to increase productivity, access, and strength. The ratchets, sockets, and wrenches are made from premium alloy steel and feature a full polish chrome finish that resists rust and corrosion. Large, hard-stamped markings allow for easy identification and can be easily recognized as SAE (double line) or metric (knurled line). Additionally, the off-corner loading design puts force on the flats of fasteners for improved durability and reduced fastener rounding.

* * *

#### WARNING:

CHOKING HAZARD -- Small parts. Not for children under 3 yrs.

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find this tool set to be a nice basic set that performs flawlessly and offers good value for money. The stroke speed receives positive feedback, with one customer noting the ratchets have a really short stroke perfect for tight spaces. The durability and carrying case receive mixed reviews - while some find it durable, others report the case breaking in multiple ways, and while some like the case, others find it terrible.

*   [![Image 180](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif) TeeJay](https://www.amazon.com/gp/profile/amzn1.account.AFLCQJXIVNJ4CPZZTYKAWVLO7HRA?ref=cm_cr_dp_d_bdcrb_top) _5 out of 5 stars_ ##### [The quality to price of this Ratchet and Socket set is amazing.](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/srp/-/R3PB92GZRTC0VV/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_ttl?_encoding=UTF8&ie=UTF8)Reviewed in the United States on March 16, 2022 [Size: One Size](https://www.amazon.com/portal/customer-reviews/B07H5H1J3X/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_fmt?_encoding=UTF8&formatType=current_format)[Verified Purchase](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_rvw_avp?ie=UTF8&nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)  Brief content visible, double tap to read full content. Full content visible, double tap to read brief content. I was looking for a good quality ratchet and socket set to replace and fix a couple of things on my car with, and to have for other jobs around the house, such as fixing bikes for my family and other odd jobs. It took me a good month before I finally settled on this set. Once the set arrived and I opened it, I was surprised to see how much this set included. Way more than I expected and actually more then I ever needed, but for the price to quality ratio, I don’t think you can beat it.

Now, Im no professional mechanic, nor am I a handyman, but I do know my way around a set of tools, and know how to use them well. With that said, I would definitely recommend this set for more of a car mechanic or a handyman that uses tools all of the time. If your like me though, and don’t use tools very often, then this set can be a little overkill. If your also like me and want to make sure you have the right tool for the job when you need it, and want to make sure you have a quality set, I would definitely recommend this set.

The only thing I would say that I probably will never use in this set, is the bigger 1/2” ratchet and sockets. I probably would have been good with the set that comes with just the 1/4” and 3/8” ratchet and sockets, but I am happy with my purchase either way.

# Product Summary: GEARWRENCH 110 Piece 6 & 12 Point SAE/Metric Mechanics Tool Set | 89058

From GEARWRENCH

4.7 out of 5 stars, 356 ratings

## Price

One-time purchase: $115.58

[Purchasing options](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B07H5H1J3X?ref=dp_product_quick_view#buybox)

## About this Item

*   Contains 12 point full polish long pattern combination wrenches and sockets
*   Sockets feature off-corner loading design to reduce fastener rounding

## Product Description

 GEARWRENCH mechanics tools have all been designed with the professional user in mind to increase productivity, access, and strength. The ratchets, sockets, and wrenches are made from premium alloy steel and feature a full polish chrome finish that resists rust and corrosion. Large, hard-stamped markings allow for easy identification and can be easily recognized as SAE (double line) or metric (knurled line). Additionally, the off-corner loading design puts force on the flats of fasteners for improved durability and reduced fastener rounding.
